# Skills · S · part 125

962 entries, shaz-audit to shell-script-validation. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **shaz-audit**: Runs the Shaz Agency 25-point universal quality gate audit on the current workspace or code to detect anti-patterns (fluff copy, pills, emojis, neon, comment leaks, layout overflows). Activated via slash command /shaz-audit. ([berkaysahin-dev/shaz-web-design-skill](https://github.com/berkaysahin-dev/shaz-web-design-skill/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/shaz-audit) · ★1)
- **shaz-component**: Generates specialized, accessible UI components from the 114 Shaz Agency NameThatUI dictionary blueprints (command palettes, segmented controls, stepped area charts, inverted pricing matrices, monumental footers) with full WAI-ARIA and zero pills. Activated via /shaz-component. ([berkaysahin-dev/shaz-web-design-skill](https://github.com/berkaysahin-dev/shaz-web-design-skill/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/shaz-component) · ★1)
- **shaz-dock**: Generates kinetic two-stage scroll-morphing collapsible dock navigation bars with deterministic CSS isolation, monochrome SVG icon array, expandable drawer, and custom SVG favicon link. Activated via /shaz-dock. ([berkaysahin-dev/shaz-web-design-skill](https://github.com/berkaysahin-dev/shaz-web-design-skill/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/shaz-dock) · ★1)
- **shaz-hero**: Generates backgroundless hero sections from 6 composition archetypes with a seeded visual centerpiece (typography, SVG illustration, generative pattern, photography, data, or WebGL when the subject is physical), direct headline primacy without eyebrows, and zero post-hero ticker strips. Activated via /shaz-hero. ([berkaysahin-dev/shaz-web-design-skill](https://github.com/berkaysahin-dev/shaz-web-design-skill/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/shaz-hero) · ★1)
- **shaz-puppet-runtime**: Animate the supplied Shaz puppet locally. Use Talk to Camera for dialogue, arrange approved body-language gestures, or repair and review one rig action without rebuilding the renderer. ([smsheik1/wiggly](https://github.com/smsheik1/wiggly/tree/HEAD/v3/public/format-repositories/shaz-puppet-runtime-v1) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **shaz-style**: Applies and transforms layouts into any of the 40 authentic Shaz Agency design styles (Swiss Utilitarian, Neobrutalism, Y2K, Acid Graphics, Bento Grid, Spatial UI, Luxury Editorial) with calibrated typography, tokens, and materials. Activated via /shaz-style. ([berkaysahin-dev/shaz-web-design-skill](https://github.com/berkaysahin-dev/shaz-web-design-skill/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/shaz-style) · ★1)

- **shcode-issue-reports**: Work through the student issue reports filed in shCode (the Report button, /teacher/issues): pull the queue, triage each report, mark it in progress, fix and verify it (tests, a real browser, the live site), deploy, then mark it fixed or deferred with a plain note the student can read. Use when asked to "address student issues", "work the issue reports", "what's left in the reports", "check the queue", "close out reports", or names a report number (#88). Also grows itself: every time the owner has to ask for a check the run should have done, it records the lesson here. ([shuff57/agent-evo](https://github.com/shuff57/agent-evo/tree/HEAD/skills/shcode-issue-reports))
